- <a href="#" id="CybotCookiebotDialogNavAbout" class="CybotCookiebotDialogNavItemLink" data-target="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentTextAbout" tabindex="-1" role="tab" aria-selected="false" aria-controls="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentTextAbout" lang="en">About</a>

Cookie Banner

This website uses cookies to enhance user experience and to analyse performance and traffic on our website. We also share information about your use of our site with our advertising and analytics partners. Some of these Cookies are necessary to help websites work properly, can’t be switched off and do not require consent, while some are optional but support us and your experience in other ways.

You can learn more about our <a href="https://www.lse.ac.uk/lse-information/privacy-policy" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><strong><strong>Privacy policy</strong></strong></a>, and manage the choices available to you at any time by clicking on "Show details".

By clicking “Accept non-essential cookies”, you agree to the storing of cookies on your device to enhance site navigation, analyse site usage, and assist in our marketing efforts.

Click on "Cookie settings" to see more information and customise your choices.

Show more

  **\_ASI_PROBE**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Set by an embedded Canva design. A short test that checks whether the browser accepts cookies, so the Canva content can load properly. Third-party; session only.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: Session</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTTP Cookie</span>

  **ASI**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">The cookie is necessary for secure log-in and the detection of any spam or abuse of the website.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: Session</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTTP Cookie</span>

  **CDI**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">The cookie is necessary for secure log-in and the detection of any spam or abuse of the website.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: 1 year</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTTP Cookie</span>

  **leadership_lock#leadership_lock**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Set by an embedded Canva design. It coordinates Canva content open in several browser tabs, so only one tab handles background tasks. Kept in the browser until cleared.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: Persistent</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: IndexedDB</span>

  **persisted_data_registry#persisted_data_registry**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Set by an embedded Canva design. It keeps a list of the data Canva has saved in the browser, so the design can load and update correctly. Kept in the browser until cleared.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: Persistent</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: IndexedDB</span>

  6

  **aws_waf_referrer**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Set by Amazon Web Services’ firewall during a security check. It saves the page the visitor came from, so they can continue to the right page after the check. Kept in the browser until cleared.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: Persistent</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTML Local Storage</span>

  **aws_waf_token_challenge_attempts**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Set by Amazon Web Services’ firewall. It counts how many times the browser has tried the security check that confirms the visitor is human, which helps protect the site from bots. Kept in the browser until cleared.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: Persistent</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTML Local Storage</span>

  **awswaf_token_refresh_timestamp**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">This cookie is used to distinguish between humans and bots. </span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: Persistent</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTML Local Storage</span>

  **chat_session**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Set by the VerbaFlo chat assistant on lse.ac.uk. It keeps the current chat conversation running while the visitor browses. Deleted when the tab is closed.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: Session</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTML Local Storage</span>

  **session_info**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Set by the VerbaFlo chat assistant. It stores details of the visitor’s chat session, so the conversation continues across pages. Kept in the browser until cleared.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: Persistent</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTML Local Storage</span>

  **socket_token**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Set by the VerbaFlo chat assistant. It stores a secure token for the live connection that sends and receives chat messages. Kept in the browser until cleared.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: Persistent</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTML Local Storage</span>

  10

  **ASP.NET_SessionId \[x2\]**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Preserves the visitor's session state across page requests.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: Session</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTTP Cookie</span>

  **ContensisSecurity \[x2\]**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Set by Contensis, the content management system behind info.lse.ac.uk. It keeps users signed in while they view restricted pages, such as staff-only pages. It’s only set for signed-in users and is deleted when the browser is closed.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: Session</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTTP Cookie</span>

  **ContensisSecurityBearerToken \[x2\]**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Set by Contensis, the content management system behind info.lse.ac.uk. It stores the secure access token that lets a signed-in user open restricted pages. It’s only set for signed-in users and is deleted when the browser is closed.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: Session</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTTP Cookie</span>

  **ContensisSecurityRefreshToken \[x2\]**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Set by Contensis, the content management system behind info.lse.ac.uk. It stores a secure token that renews the user’s sign-in without asking them to log in again. It’s only set for signed-in users and is deleted when the browser is closed.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: Session</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTTP Cookie</span>

  **HasContensisSecurity \[x2\]**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Set by Contensis, the content management system behind info.lse.ac.uk. It records whether the user is signed in, so restricted content displays correctly. It’s only set for signed-in users and is deleted when the browser is closed</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: Session</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTTP Cookie</span>

  **redirect**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Set by LSE Student Hub (studenthub.lse.ac.uk).   <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: Persistent</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTML Local Storage</span>

  **redirectAction**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Set by LSE Student Hub (studenthub.lse.ac.uk). Kept in local storage until it is cleared or overwritten.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: Persistent</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTML Local Storage</span>

<span id="CybotCookiebotDialogBodyLevelButtonPreferencesInlineLabel" role="heading" aria-level="3">Preferences </span> <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBulkConsentCount">29</span>

<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogBodyLevelButtonSlider" aria-hidden="true"></span>

Preference cookies enable a website to remember information that changes the way the website behaves or looks, like your preferred language or the region that you are in.

  **analytics-clock-skew**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Set by an embedded Canva design. It records the time difference between the visitor’s device and Canva’s servers, so Canva’s usage data is timed accurately. Kept in the browser until cleared.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: Persistent</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTML Local Storage</span>

  **dv_last_interaction**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Stores data on the time spent on the website and its sub-pages, during the current session. </span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: Persistent</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTML Local Storage</span>

  **analytics_event_v2#analytics_event_metadata**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Allows queries from third-party database for implementation of visual content. </span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: Persistent</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: IndexedDB</span>

  **analytics_event_v2#analytics_event_v2**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Allows queries from third-party database for implementation of visual content. </span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: Persistent</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: IndexedDB</span>

  **canva-main-offline#queue**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Set by an embedded Canva design. It holds error and diagnostic reports while offline, so they can be sent to Canva’s error-monitoring service later. Kept in the browser until cleared.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: Persistent</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: IndexedDB</span>

  **CCDA**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Set by an embedded Canva design. Canva uses it for its own measurement of how embedded designs are used. Third-party; session only.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: Session</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTTP Cookie</span>

- <a href="#" class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieProvider CybotCookiebotDialogCollapsed" role="button" aria-expanded="false">Google</a>

  17

  <a href="https://business.safety.google/privacy/" class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieLink" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer nofollow" aria-label="Learn more about this provider - opens in a new window" title="Learn more about this provider Google&#39;s privacy policy - opens in a new window">Learn more about this provider<img src="data:image/png;base64,iVBORw0KGgoAAAANSUhEUgAAABIAAAASCAYAAABWzo5XAAAACXBIWXMAAAsTAAALEwEAmpwYAAAAAXNSR0IArs4c6QAAAARnQU1BAACxjwv8YQUAAAC6SURBVHgBpdTdCcMgEAdwT/vk04EOkBG6QifpHH3rCF2hk3SVvBaEuoDYu4IgRRMv/iEkD/Hn+QnOuatS6gEAqDoJIQC/vfe594+m576FjAZKL6XXkSDiYox5UQFLXZEoNZJzXg9B/0hK6SKGWkiMcRVBewjnNIuURdIzyFBFo0jZPnoGqaMbCEqRJkSNIiFPCfILj7F1GLkySfvuqnFlShDxWesF6D76zF4jPJ/GWvum7/NRjBCegtsXQuuTY/odOJIAAAAASUVORK5CYII=" class="CybotExternalLinkArrow" alt="opens in a new window" /></a>

  Some of the data collected by this provider is for the purposes of personalization and measuring advertising effectiveness. The provider may use the IP Addresses for ads measurement and ads personalization.

  **\_ga \[x5\]**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Used to send data to Google Analytics about the visitor's device and behavior. Tracks the visitor across devices and marketing channels.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: 2 years</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTTP Cookie</span>

  **\_ga\_# \[x5\]**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Used to send data to Google Analytics about the visitor's device and behavior. Tracks the visitor across devices and marketing channels.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: 2 years</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTTP Cookie</span>

  **\_\_utm.gif**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Google Analytics Tracking Code that logs details about the visitor's browser and computer.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: Session</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: Pixel Tracker</span>

  **\_\_utma**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Collects data on the number of times a user has visited the website as well as dates for the first and most recent visit. Used by Google Analytics.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: 2 years</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTTP Cookie</span>

  **\_\_utmb**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Registers a timestamp with the exact time of when the user accessed the website. Used by Google Analytics to calculate the duration of a website visit.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: 1 day</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTTP Cookie</span>

  **\_\_utmc**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Registers a timestamp with the exact time of when the user leaves the website. Used by Google Analytics to calculate the duration of a website visit.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: Session</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTTP Cookie</span>

  **\_\_utmt**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Used to throttle the speed of requests to the server.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: 1 day</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTTP Cookie</span>

  **\_\_utmv**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Saves user-defined tracking parameters for use in Google Analytics.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: Session</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTTP Cookie</span>

  **\_\_utmz**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Collects data on where the user came from, what search engine was used, what link was clicked and what search term was used. Used by Google Analytics.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: 6 months</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTTP Cookie</span>

  **\_cs_root-domain \[x3\]**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Registers how the user has reached the website to enable pay-out of referral commission fees to partners.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: Session</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTTP Cookie</span>

  **\_hjTLDTest \[x3\]**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Registers statistical data on users' behaviour on the website. Used for internal analytics by the website operator. </span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: Session</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTTP Cookie</span>

  **\_cs_same_site \[x4\]**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Registers data on visitors' website-behaviour. This is used for internal analysis and website optimization. </span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: Session</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTTP Cookie</span>

  **\_cs_ex \[x2\]**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Set by Contentsquare, a behaviour analytics and session replay tool. It marks the visitor as excluded from Contentsquare tracking, for example by sampling or an exclusion rule, so that no data is collected about them. First-party, expires after 30 days.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: 30 days</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTTP Cookie</span>

  **\_hjSession\_# \[x3\]**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Collects statistics on the visitor's visits to the website, such as the number of visits, average time spent on the website and what pages have been read.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: 1 day</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTTP Cookie</span>

  **\_hjSessionUser\_# \[x3\]**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Collects statistics on the visitor's visits to the website, such as the number of visits, average time spent on the website and what pages have been read.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: 1 year</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTTP Cookie</span>

  **\_hjUserAttributes**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Registers statistical data on users' behaviour on the website. Used for internal analytics by the website operator. </span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: Persistent</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTML Local Storage</span>

  **hjActiveViewportIds**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">This cookie contains an ID string on the current session. This contains non-personal information on what subpages the visitor enters – this information is used to optimize the visitor's experience.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: Persistent</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTML Local Storage</span>

  **hjViewportId**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Saves the user's screen size in order to adjust the size of images on the website.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: Session</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTML Local Storage</span>

  **\_cs_id**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Registers statistical data on users' behaviour on the website. Used for internal analytics by the website operator. </span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: 13 months</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTTP Cookie</span>

  **\_cs_s**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">This cookie is used to identify the frequency of visits and how long the visitor is on the website. The cookie is also used to determine how many and which subpages the visitor visits on a website – this information can be used by the website to optimize the domain and its subpages.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: 1 day</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTTP Cookie</span>

  **\_hjHasCachedUserAttributes**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Identifies if the cookie data needs to be updated in the visitor's browser.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: Session</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTTP Cookie</span>

  **\_hjCookieTest**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Collects data on the user’s navigation and behavior on the website. This is used to compile statistical reports and heatmaps for the website owner.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: Session</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTTP Cookie</span>

  **capi_id**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Set by GetSmarter on Executive Education course pages. It stores an ID used to send visit and conversion data to advertising platforms (Conversions API) to measure ad campaigns. Third-party; session only.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: Session</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTTP Cookie</span>

- <a href="#" class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieProvider CybotCookiebotDialogCollapsed" role="button" aria-expanded="false">Google</a>

  10

  <a href="https://business.safety.google/privacy/" class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieLink" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer nofollow" aria-label="Learn more about this provider - opens in a new window" title="Learn more about this provider Google&#39;s privacy policy - opens in a new window">Learn more about this provider<img src="data:image/png;base64,iVBORw0KGgoAAAANSUhEUgAAABIAAAASCAYAAABWzo5XAAAACXBIWXMAAAsTAAALEwEAmpwYAAAAAXNSR0IArs4c6QAAAARnQU1BAACxjwv8YQUAAAC6SURBVHgBpdTdCcMgEAdwT/vk04EOkBG6QifpHH3rCF2hk3SVvBaEuoDYu4IgRRMv/iEkD/Hn+QnOuatS6gEAqDoJIQC/vfe594+m576FjAZKL6XXkSDiYox5UQFLXZEoNZJzXg9B/0hK6SKGWkiMcRVBewjnNIuURdIzyFBFo0jZPnoGqaMbCEqRJkSNIiFPCfILj7F1GLkySfvuqnFlShDxWesF6D76zF4jPJ/GWvum7/NRjBCegtsXQuuTY/odOJIAAAAASUVORK5CYII=" class="CybotExternalLinkArrow" alt="opens in a new window" /></a>

  Some of the data collected by this provider is for the purposes of personalization and measuring advertising effectiveness. The provider may use the IP Addresses for ads measurement and ads personalization.

  **\_gcl_au \[x3\]**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Used to measure the efficiency of the website’s advertisement efforts, by collecting data on the conversion rate of the website’s ads across multiple websites.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: 3 months</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTTP Cookie</span>

  **IDE**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Used by Google DoubleClick to register and report the website user's actions after viewing or clicking one of the advertiser's ads with the purpose of measuring the efficacy of an ad and to present targeted ads to the user.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: 400 days</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTTP Cookie</span>

  **NID**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Registers a unique ID that identifies a returning user's device. The ID is used for targeted ads.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: 6 months</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTTP Cookie</span>

  **pagead/1p-user-list/#**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Tracks if the user has shown interest in specific products or events across multiple websites and detects how the user navigates between sites. This is used for measurement of advertisement efforts and facilitates payment of referral-fees between websites.</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: Session</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: Pixel Tracker</span>

  **ccm/conversion/704614692/**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Pending</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: Session</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: Pixel Tracker</span>

  **ddm/activity/src=#**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Pending</span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: Session</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: Pixel Tracker</span>

  **\_gcl_ls \[x2\]**<span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoDescription">Tracks the conversion rate between the user and the advertisement banners on the website - This serves to optimise the relevance of the advertisements on the website. </span>

  <span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Maximum Storage Duration**: Persistent</span><span class="CybotCookiebotDialogDetailBodyContentCookieInfoFooterContent">**Type**: HTML Local Storage</span>

You can at any time change or withdraw your consent from the Cookie Declaration on our website.

# Researchfish

##  Information for LSE Principal Investigators who are required to report annually against the outcomes and impacts of their funded research.

### **Researchfish 2026 submission deadline: Thursday 12 March 2026, 4pm GMT** 

 

<a href="https://app.researchfish.com/user/login?destination=awards" class="cta--secondary external" onclick="void(window.open(&#39;https://app.researchfish.com/user/login?destination=awards&#39;,&#39;&#39;,&#39;toolbar=yes,menubar=yes,location=yes,scrollbars=yes,status=yes,resizable=yes&#39;));return false;" onkeypress="void(window.open(&#39;https://app.researchfish.com/user/login?destination=awards&#39;,&#39;&#39;,&#39;toolbar=yes,menubar=yes,location=yes,scrollbars=yes,status=yes,resizable=yes&#39;));return false;" title="Login to Researchfish here">Login to Researchfish here </a>

## What is Researchfish? 

<a href="https://researchfish.com/" class="sys_16 external">Researchfish</a> is an external online system that collects details of research outcomes for a range of funders. Principal Investigators (PIs) who receive awards from these funders are required to report on the **impacts and outcomes** of their research via Researchfish during an **annual 6-week submission period**. 

Reporting requirements vary per funder. For example, PIs of UKRI awards are currently required to complete an annual submission in Researchfish for the **duration of their award and a minimum of 5 years post the award end date**. For information on how often you will be required to report on your award, please refer to the [relevant funder webpages](#fg).

## How will I know if I need to report against my award(s)?

As a PI, if you have an award you need to report against for the first time, you will receive an invite from Researchfish via email with instructions on how to register your account. You will then receive communications from both the associated funder and LSE Research and Innovation with further details on how and when to complete your submission(s) and where to seek assistance if required.  

It is the **PI’s responsibility** to ensure that their annual submission is made during the annual submission window. PI’s who do not submit their annual report by the deadline will be subject to funder sanctions. Non-compliance by individuals will also result in sanctions being applied to the institution (e.g. grant payments withheld). 

## Key Dates

The 2026 Researchfish submission period opens on Monday 2 February 2026 and closes on **Thursday 12 March 2026, 16:00 GMT**.

This is a fixed deadline, and any submissions received after this time will be marked as late and non-compliant.

We **strongly advise** that you do not wait until the deadline day to complete your submission as it is common for a queue to form, and you risk not meeting the deadline.

## Oversight of Researchfish submissions by the Research and Innovation Division

Communications and support for the annual Researchfish submission period are managed by <a href="mailto:c.a.creamore@lse.ac.uk" class="sys_16">Charlotte Creamore</a> in the Research and Innovation Division. Charlotte monitors and maintains a record of researcher compliance throughout the submission period and sends regular reminder emails to those with outstanding submissions.

If you are a **unit/research manager** and would like to be copied into these communications, please contact <a href="mailto:c.a.creamore@lse.ac.uk" class="sys_16">Charlotte</a>. 

## Who should I contact for support?

**Researchfish system queries**

Any technical issues with the Researchfish system should be directed to <a href="mailto:support@researchfish.com" class="sys_16">support@researchfish.com</a>. 

**Non-technical/policy queries**

For UKRI awardees, please direct any non-technical or policy questions to <a href="mailto:researchoutcomes@funding.ukri.org" class="sys_16">researchoutcomes@funding.ukri.org</a>.

For NIHR awardees, please direct any non-technical or policy questions to <a href="mailto:outcomesreporting@nihr.ac.uk" class="sys_16">outcomesreporting@nihr.ac.uk</a>. *Please note that the NIHR email address has changed since the last submission period.*

**Other**

Please reach out to <a href="mailto:c.a.creamore@lse.ac.uk" class="sys_16">Charlotte Creamore</a> for all other queries relating to the Researchfish submission period. 

## <span id="fg"></span>Additional funder guidance

For further details and guidance on reporting against **UKRI** awards, including a list of common output types, please click <a href="https://www.ukri.org/manage-your-award/reporting-your-projects-outcomes/what-you-need-to-report-and-how-to-do-it" class="sys_16 external">here</a>.

For further details and guidance on reporting against **NIHR** awards, please click <a href="https://www.nihr.ac.uk/documents/nihr-researchfish-guidance/12294" class="sys_16 external">here</a>.

## FAQs 

<a href="one" class="accordion__trigger"></a>

## I have received an email reminding me to complete my annual Researchfish submission. Do I still need to complete a submission if:  - My award has finished, and I have submitted my final report to the funder?  - My award has not produced any outputs since the last submission period?  - My award started very recently and there are no outputs to report?

Yes, you are still required to complete a submission. In these instances, you are required to submit what is known as a null response. You are required to complete each of the mandatory fields, signalled by a red '! Required' symbol. 

The mandatory fields are made up of free text and multiple-choice questions. If you have nothing to report in the free text sections, please write a short piece of narrative explaining that there have been no new impacts or outcomes from your award. When answering the multiple-choice questions, please select the most appropriate answer. 

<a href="one" class="accordion__trigger"></a>

## How do I know which section of the Researchfish submission form I should submit a specific outcome under?

Please refer to the drop-downs titled 'What should I report in this section?' that can be found in each section of the submission form.

For further guidance on categorisation of outputs, please refer to the <a href="https://rf-downloads.s3.amazonaws.com/Outcome+type+map.pdf" class="sys_16 external" onclick="void(window.open(&#39;https://rf-downloads.s3.amazonaws.com/Outcome+type+map.pdf&#39;,&#39;&#39;,&#39;toolbar=yes,menubar=yes,location=yes,scrollbars=yes,status=yes,resizable=yes&#39;));return false;" onkeypress="void(window.open(&#39;https://rf-downloads.s3.amazonaws.com/Outcome+type+map.pdf&#39;,&#39;&#39;,&#39;toolbar=yes,menubar=yes,location=yes,scrollbars=yes,status=yes,resizable=yes&#39;));return false;">list of common output types</a>. 

<a href="one" class="accordion__trigger"></a>

## This year’s Researchfish submission form has different fields than previous years. Why is that?

As part of a drive to minimise undue burden when reporting outcomes, funders are prioritising collecting data that is of most use to them. Please refer to the individual funder webpages for news on changes to the information they collect via Researchfish:

**NIHR **– <a href="https://www.nihr.ac.uk/nihr-researchfish-guidance#:~:text=activity%20or%20impact-,Why%20NIHR%20uses%20Researchfish%20for%20impact%20reporting,Changes%20to%20the%202026%20submission%20period,-NIHR%20continues%20to" class="sys_16 external">Changes to the 2026 submission period</a>

**UKRI **– <a href="https://www.ukri.org/manage-your-award/reporting-your-projects-outcomes/what-you-need-to-report/#:~:text=Improvements%20to%20the%20question%20sets" class="sys_16 external">Improvements to the question sets</a>

<a href="one" class="accordion__trigger"></a>

## I have a very busy workload at the moment. Please can one of my research team complete the submission for me?

You can delegate access to your award to members of your research team, allowing them to complete certain sections of the form. Please click <a href="https://rf-downloads.s3.amazonaws.com/RF-PI-UserGuide-2022.pdf" class="sys_16 external" target="_blank">here</a> for guidance on how to do this. 

However, please note that only you as the PI can:

- Complete the mandatory ‘Additional Funder Questions’.
- Press the final ‘Submit’ button to complete the submission. 

<a href="one" class="accordion__trigger"></a>

## I don’t have time to complete my submission. Please can I be granted an extension?

The submission deadline of Thursday 12 March 2026 4pm GMT is fixed, and **extensions cannot be granted**.

<a href="one" class="accordion__trigger"></a>

## In what circumstances could I be granted a 1-year exemption?

PIs may be granted a 1-year exemption because of long-term leave by agreement with the funder (i.e. maternity leave, long-term parental leave, long-term sick leave). Requests for an exemption are assessed by the funder on a case-by-case basis. If you believe you should be granted an exemption, please reach out to <a href="mailto:c.a.creamore@lse.ac.uk" class="sys_16">Charlotte Creamore</a> as soon as possible. 

<a href="one" class="accordion__trigger"></a>

## I am expecting new outcomes and/or impacts of my award(s) to materialise shortly after the close of the submission period. Should I include these expected outcomes/impacts in my submission?

The Researchfish system is open year-round for data entry. Researchers must only report on realised impacts and outcomes. 

<a href="one" class="accordion__trigger"></a>

## The Researchfish reminder email I received from Research and Innovation states that I have X number of awards to report against, but I can only see Y number of awards on my account. What should I do?

You will have received separate invites from each Council from which you have received an award. Please ensure that you have accepted each invite you have received. Unless you link each award to your account, you will not be able to provide your final submission.

<a href="one" class="accordion__trigger"></a>

## What happens if I miss the deadline?

If you miss the Researchfish submission deadline, you will face the following sanctions:

- Losing your eligibility to be the PI, or a named co-investigator, on any future projects funded by the respective funder.
- Suspension of funding payments for current awards for which you are PI.

<a href="one" class="accordion__trigger"></a>

## I understand that Researchfish will be decommissioned by July 2027. How will I be expected to report on the impacts and outcomes of my research following this date?

Elsevier, the owner of Researchfish, will be decommissioning the system on Saturday 31 July 2027. After this point, Elsevier does not plan to supply a like-for-like alternative, which means that it is up to each individual funder to decide how they would like to collect research outcomes from their awardees. A competitor provider may wish to supply an alternative, or the funder community may wish to take a different approach. This webpage will be updated once further information is available.
